- **evidence-argument-chain**: 当需要将案件中的多项证据与法律论证主张进行系统性对应挂钩时触发此技能。 典型触发场景包括：准备诉讼文书（起诉状、答辩状、代理词）时需要构建完整的证据-主张对应关系； 庭审前整理证据目录并明确每项证据的证明目的；对方提出反驳后需要重新组织证据链以回应； 案件涉及多个争议焦点，需要将证据分组归类到不同论证主张下； 需要评估现有证据是否足以支撑全部主张，识别证据链中的薄弱环节和缺口。 本技能的核心是建立"主张→要件→证据→证明力评估"的完整映射关系，确保每个法律主张都有充分的证据支撑， 每项证据都有明确的证明目的，整体论证链条逻辑严密、层次清晰。 ([THUYRan/Legal-Skills-Chinese](https://github.com/THUYRan/Legal-Skills-Chinese/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-argument-chain) · ★985 · 4 copies)

- **evidence-argument-chain**: 用于将案件中的各项证据与法律主张系统性地对应挂钩、构建完整证据链时触发。 典型触发场景： - 构建"证据-主张"对应关系，把每项证据归位到具体的法律主张与构成要件之下； - 庭审前编制证据目录并逐项写明证明目的； - 对方提出反驳或异议后，需要重新组织、重组证据链以回应； - 案件存在多个争议焦点，需按焦点将证据分组归类； - 评估现有证据是否足以支撑全部主张，识别证据链薄弱环节与缺口并给出补强建议。 核心是建立"主张 → 要件 → 证据 → 证明力评估"的完整映射，确保每个主张都有充分证据、每项证据都有明确证明目的。 不适用于：单纯的证据真伪/三性鉴别（仅审单份证据可信度时无须建链）、法律检索或法条释义、 文书全文起草排版、事实时间线梳理（无主张-要件映射需求时）、以及非中国大陆法语境的案件。 ([FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon](https://github.com/FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon/tree/HEAD/hongzhi-core/skills/evidence-argument-chain) · ★2)

- **evidence-assessment**: Evaluating the quality, provenance, and relevance of evidence that supports or undermines a claim. - **evidence-based-writing**: 日本語を中心とする研究文書、技術文書、調査報告、解説、仕様書、提案書などを作成・推敲し、中心命題への寄与、論理関係、主張範囲、用語、文の処理負荷を改善する。文章品質の改善、リライト、推敲、校正、簡潔化、論理構成の整理、曖昧表現の修正を求められたときに使用する。創作的な文体変更より、正確で明確な説明文を必要とするタスクを優先する。 ([minaph/evidence-based-writing](https://github.com/minaph/evidence-based-writing/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **evidence-bearing-test-design**: Designs a focused executable observation that distinguishes intended behavior from a consequential plausible fault. Applies when a test oracle, fixture, observation layer, failure meaning, or evidence boundary needs definition or repair. ([driftframestudios/human-ai-systems-exemplars](https://github.com/driftframestudios/human-ai-systems-exemplars/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/evidence-bearing-test-design) · 2 copies)
- **evidence-before-after**: Use to build the before-and-after visual evidence for a Zava Learning incident. First classify the fault, then render the ONE visual that actually explains what changed — a before/after path or topology diagram for connectivity/config/RBAC faults, or time-series comparison charts for performance/availability faults — plus a state/metric delta table. Never plot a metric that does not tell the story. Its output is used in the RCA and the zava-reporting deliverables. ([microsoft/sre-agent](https://github.com/microsoft/sre-agent/tree/HEAD/labs/zava-learning/sre-config/agent-config/skills/evidence-before-after) · ★194 · 3 copies)
- **evidence-before-claim**: Use before writing any sentence that claims work is done, fixed, passing, or green — and when a completion report is challenged. Triggers: "gates green", "tests pass", "all checks pass", "done", "fixed", warden reports "claims checks passed but none ran", "reports completion after N file changes with no test/build/lint". Enforces that every claim in a report is backed by command output produced in the same turn, and that the checker actually covers the change (right language, right linter, right config). ([danieldekay/ai-ops](https://github.com/danieldekay/ai-ops/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claim) · ★1)
- **evidence-before-claim**: Pointer to the canonical Evidence Before Claim skill. Read evidence-before-claim/SKILL.md. This file is not a second copy. ([kazoya/rctc-skill](https://github.com/kazoya/rctc-skill/tree/HEAD/docs))
- **evidence-before-claim**: Produce the evidence before stating that something works, and separate what was proven from what is still assumed. Use for "هل تم فعلاً؟"، "تحقق قبل أن تقول تم"، bug reports whose cause is not yet known, fixes that need a counterfactual, and any report that will be read as a guarantee. Do not use for exploratory work where nothing is being claimed yet. ([kazoya/rctc-skill](https://github.com/kazoya/rctc-skill/tree/HEAD/evidence-before-claim))
- **evidence-before-claims**: Evidence gate for security research, scanner triage, code review, and reporting. Use before confirming vulnerability impact, auth material, control results, cleanup, or root cause. ([AeonDave/malskill](https://github.com/AeonDave/malskill/tree/HEAD/behaviours/evidence-before-claims) · ★42)
- **evidence-before-claims**: Use immediately before claiming work is complete, fixed, passing, committed, published, or ready for handoff; require fresh verification evidence matched to the claim. ([sandbaseai/workbuddy-skill](https://github.com/sandbaseai/workbuddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **evidence-before-claims**: Verification gate before any claim of success. Use right before saying anything is done, fixed, working, passing, faster, safe, or complete, before committing, pushing, or opening a PR, before moving to the next task, and before reporting a subagent's results. Requires fresh output from the command that proves each claim (tests, build, type-check, lint, reproduction of the original symptom, benchmark), and honest reporting of anything not verified. ([milad-raeisi/senior-skills](https://github.com/milad-raeisi/senior-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims) · ★4)
- **evidence-before-claims**: Use before claiming ANYTHING works, passes, is fixed, or is done — before every commit, PR, task hand-off, or "it should work now". Triggers on the urge to say done, or words like "should", "probably", "that fixes it", "all green". Not for writing the failing test that drives new code — that's test-first ([atgreen/hackinator](https://github.com/atgreen/hackinator/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims) · ★1)
- **evidence-before-claims**: Discipline. Verify before saying something is true, and say what wasn't verified. Applied by the orchestrators in this package before stating a cause, declaring a fix effective or reporting a result. ([rbadillap/sre](https://github.com/rbadillap/sre/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims))
- **evidence-before-claims**: Checks version-dependent and external facts against a source before stating them: APIs, flags, defaults, standards, statutes, subagent findings, and the status, authorship and security claims a README or spec makes before a push. Labels each claim verified, reported or inferred. Use when an answer depends on a dependency or API, even when it seems known, before citing a CVE, RFC or law, or before docs are pushed. Not for logic bugs. ([Rene-Roscher/skills](https://github.com/Rene-Roscher/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/evidence-before-claims))
- **evidence-before-completion**: Use when a hub or specialist has specific completion claims to verify. Map each claim to fresh proof output before saying work is done, fixed, or ready. - **evidence-before-done**: 多助手协作底座。任何助手准备说「做好了」「修好了」「放好了」「没问题」「都对上了」， 或要交付、在进度里说某部分已完成、进入下一步、转述别的助手或子任务的「已完成」之前用： 先拿到对得上当前版本、范围、环境的有效证据（打开看、播放、重算、按原步骤再走一遍、逐条对照原要求）； 本人或指定核验人对同一版本的有效核验可复用，有变更或失败只补核受影响部分；执行人自己的报告不算独立核验。 只报进行到哪的普通进度不触发全套重验。证据撑得住才下结论，撑不住就照实报现状。 不用于：第三方独立复核清单（另用审核类技能）； 动手前讲方案（另用方案确认类技能）。与同仓 ../dispatch-accept-verify/ 分工：那支管收回来，本支管核过再宣称完成。 ([dabingfy-dot/solo-to-team-skills](https://github.com/dabingfy-dot/solo-to-team-skills/tree/HEAD/bundles/bundle1/packages/Solo-to-Team-deepseek-bundle1/foundation/skills/evidence-before-done) · ★1.1k · 5 copies)

- **evidence-before-done**: Blocks the agent from claiming a task is done, fixed, verified, ready, or complete unless that claim is backed by concrete evidence (a real command actually run, with its real output) or an explicit list of what was NOT verified. - **evidence-before-done**: Never claim work is done, fixed, passing or deployed without proof: the exact command that was run and the relevant lines of its output.
